[[Laughers from the wing of SYRIZA]] [[Applause from the New Democracy wing]] Take good care, then, when you talk about PPC. Because behind this incredible disaster, which you have caused, many responsibilities lie behind it. I don't have to say anything more. Several Members have spoken without even having heard what I had said, having ready-made speeches, without even taking into account my position and the tuna that I wanted to give to the debate. And some, few are, MPs wanted to invest their objections on so-called ideological criteria. So, class policies have again been remembered, they have resurrected the so-called neoliberalism of technocrats. They fell out. They were the same arguments that you used before the elections on 7 July and the citizens crushed them in the poll. The New Democracy of 2019 recommends to the Greeks a really progressive path for the future of the country and that is now our role. The people's mandate 15 days ago was a clear and mature one, to change course with the past and leave the management of misery behind, because the times have changed and the ideological differences are obviously not abolished. Under pressure, however, their borders are shifting. Because reality is becoming more and more complex, especially in countries that have been left behind for a long time, like ours. For example, at times the issue of security has indeed been an argument to impose the winners on the losers. Indeed, there have been times in Greek history that has been the pretext of power to consolidate itself against democracy. Today, however, the same subject has changed to a central demand of the daily citizen and, indeed, the weakest citizen, the oldest woman who wants to go around her neighborhood, the merchant in the center of Athens, who wants his shop not to be the raid of robbers, the student who wants to study freely at his school and the retired person who just wants to sleep in peace every night at home. The same dramatic changes are happening in the economy. No-one, centre-right, centre-left, liberal, socialist is questioning the need for a fair redistribution of the national product, and prosperity must be for everyone and not just a few. However, there is a good argument that there should be some sort of reductions in legislation in order to get there somewhere. The problem of the country is how to produce much more new wealth, so that it can then be distributed fairly. The growth, therefore, is strong growth as the new limit between maintenance and progress. Because, with stagnation, there are always the established interests, namely those who want to maintain their position of power and their prerogatives in new circumstances. In other words, Greece is changing, changing from below, society itself. And the New Democracy comes along with currents that are now clear majority in Greek society, to become the dominant political expression of those who are asking to make a bold leap in the future, to give substance to the term "progressive". And to those who rush to learn ideological controversy from the past centuries, I suggest attention. Their word is in danger of echoing as an echo from a time when it has passed unreturned. They're in danger of no one listening anymore. We are not in front of our times when we talk about technological revolution, about climate change. Just some others stayed behind and didn't get it. [[Applause from the New Democracy wing]] Ladies and gentlemen, this must be the perspective of the new Greece, a view in which they bury the doctrines of the past and clearly highlight the crucial issue of the times, and that is just one, the tangible result. History, after all, teaches that when the Greeks assembled around such a great goal, we were magnified. It is the liberal modernization, which Eleftherios Venizelos served first, a regeneration current that will eventually highlight the good side of each of us. And that is the concept of the New Government's Programmatic Declarations: Unity, plan, rules, work and, above all, practical solutions. To conclude, I would like to explain this last report, with a specific timely one, but also tragically painful ones. Tomorrow's one year from the national tragedy in Matty. Our state then proved not only incapable of protecting so many lives and property, it turns out, unfortunately, that it is unable even now to heal some of the major wounds. EUR 37.8 million has been deposited in the special firefighting account so far by two thousand seven hundred natural or legal persons. One year later, however, 31 million are pouring - yes, they are ponding - with the first few days of disbursement being made before the elections. At the same time, of the four hundred seventy-five buildings to be demolished, only sixty-one were demolished and this was on the initiative of construction companies. Many of the affected want to buy another real estate, but they cannot, because the demolition must be completed in order to receive the aid. The deforestation of asbestos, the cleaning of land in New Buja and the projects of the region other than the anti-corrosions that have gone ahead, as well as many projects of the central administration. Of the two thousand four hundred thirty rehabilitation applications, only four hundred forty were issued. We already have a full picture of the situation, a picture of which, of course, cannot be prided by anyone who had power up to two weeks ago. Our answer wants to honor the memory of the hundreds of victims, give a substantial hand to help the affected and yes, learn from the mistakes of the past, but above all send a message that the state can function differently. We can raise our heads, we can bring results, we can be better. Therefore, firstly, by means of a legislative act, immediately after today's vote, the central administration is able to intervene in circumstances of natural disasters. And this will allow direct high-policy moves, using the special account's money, with the first move to clean up this land in which a huge amount of fuel has been collected... [[Applause from the New Democracy wing]] ... for which the General Secretariat of the Government has already contacted all major construction companies and has asked them to clean up the relevant plot without any charge for the Greek State, under corporate social responsibility. [[Applause from the New Democracy wing]] In the same legislative act, it is set up as a new body, a non-profit-making company. It participates with their representatives, first of all, the great volunteers of local society, whom I salute and thank from the heart for their devotion to their work. [[Applause from the New Democracy wing]] The Court of Auditors, the General Accounting Office of the State, the Bank of Greece, the Technical Chamber, the Ministry of the Environment and Infrastructure and the General Secretariat of the Government shall participate. And the mission of this new body will be to draw up an action plan to restore the area in Matti, New Buja and Kineta - we have forgotten about it, because it has also been affected - and the suggestions made by this body will constitute the operational renewal plan for the area that will enter into force immediately. And yes, we trust the local community in the area's livelihood in Matti to point to us where the donor's money should be directed. That means change from below up. That means trusting the citizens who, above all, know their own problems. [[Applause from the New Democracy wing]] Thirdly, the Ministry of Health has already ensured that our fellow citizens, with heavy burns, do not only have access to medicines - they already had access to them - but also to any other medical provision, which, unfortunately, was not covered by the EEA regulation. We're talking about 50 and eight of our fellow citizens. Many practically saved the teeth. For many intensified people it was certain that they would not survive, yet, they were able to live and live today their own separate drama. They don't just have burns that will accompany them all their lives, they have great scars in their souls. And the least we can do is, with some simple moves, stand by them and tell them that they have the right to hope that they will rebuild their lives. And fourthly, we are entrusting the TEI with the priority of a new pilot project for integration in the region in order to solve problems of decades. Under normal circumstances, that would take from three to five years. I request that it be done within a year and completed before the second black anniversary of the tragedy in Matty. Ladies and gentlemen, I have chosen to close my speech not by general declarations, as usual, but by talking about something very specific, speaking about Matty. Because that is precisely what symbolizes the way to rectify, with the participation of all of us on the basis of a planned plan. This is the way Greece is being called today to follow, with a catalyst's role, which will effectively and dynamically trigger all the creative forces of the country. The country is facing a huge challenge today, overcoming outdated beliefs and uniting itself in a large flow of democracy, development and modernisation, in a wave of creation and regeneration in the name of its own future. And it is up to all of us, in the hands of all our citizens, to be able to achieve this, so that we ourselves can write our own history, starting from today, in voting for the government's programming declarations. I am convinced that we will succeed for the bright Greece that we all deserve! Thank you very much.
